<p>
    Nikolai Gogol ist ein erfolgloser Schriftsteller, der sich seinen Lebensunterhalt bei der Sankt Petersburger Polizei mit der Dokumentation von Mordfällen verdient. Seit seiner Kindheit wird er von heftigen Anfällen geplagt, bei denen er Visionen hat, die nun immer häufiger an den Tatorten auftreten und zur Aufklärung der Fälle beitragen...

<b>Aktuellste Blockbuster &amp; Serien. Kein Abo.</b><br/>
Videoload ist die Online-Videothek der Telekom mit über 20.000 aktuellsten <a href="//www.videoload.de/filme/neue-filme" title="Aktuelle Filme">Filmen</a> und <a href="//www.videoload.de/serien/neue-serien" title="Aktuelle Serien">Serienepisoden</a> zum Mieten oder Kaufen – ohne Abo. Auf <a href="//www.videoload.de" title="Online Videothek">www.videoload.de</a> erhalten Sie Blockbuster, frisch aus dem Kino, noch vor DVD-Start sowie Serien direkt nach TV-Ausstrahlung in den USA, in Großbritannien oder Deutschland. Auf Wunsch sehen Sie alle Filme in <a href="//www.videoload.de/filme/high-definition" title="Film in HD">HD</a>, über 300 sogar schon in gestochen scharfem <a href="//www.videoload.de/filme/uhd" title="Film in Ultra HD 4K">Ultra HD</a> (UHD), und Dolby 5.1 Raumklang. Bei über 15.000 Inhalten wechseln Sie außerdem kostenfrei zur Originalsprache.
<br/><br/>
<b>Filme, schon ab 99 Cent</b><br/>
Bei Videoload können Sie schon ab 99 Cent <a href="//www.videoload.de/filme" title="Filme online">Filme</a> online sehen – einzelne Serienepisoden ab nur 1,49 Euro. Bis zu 85 Prozent sparen Sie, wenn Sie die komplette Staffel kaufen.
<br/><br/>
<b>Zuhause &amp; unterwegs – auch ohne Internet</b><br/>
Bequem auf dem Fernseher sehen Sie Ihre Inhalte per <a href="//www.videoload.de/chromecast">Google Chromecast</a>-Stick oder per App für <a href="//www.videoload.de/smart-tv">Smart TVs</a> von Samsung, LG, Philips, Sony und Panasonic. Auch über <a href="//www.videoload.de/magentatv">MagentaTV</a> von der Telekom ist Videoload im Wohnzimmer per Media Receiver oder Amazon Fire TV verfügbar. Unterwegs auf dem <a href="//www.videoload.de/mobil">Smartphone</a> oder <a href="//www.videoload.de/mobil">Tablet</a> mit Android oder iOS Betriebssystem sehen Sie Ihre <a href="//www.videoload.de/filme" title="Filme online">Filme</a> und <a href="//www.videoload.de/serien" title="Serien online">Serien</a> direkt im Browser oder – auch ohne Internet-Verbindung – über die <a href="//www.videoload.de/smartphone-tablet">MagentaTV App</a>.

<!-- ENDE ATI Tracking-Pixel -->
<script type="text/javascript">
/**
 * This file contains functions used to manipulate the "cookie-banner" and
 * elements depending on it.
 */
$(function() {

	function showBanner() {
		// Show cookie banner
		$(".cookie-banner").addClass("visible");
		// set marker on body
		$("body:first").addClass("banner-activated");

		fixTopMargin();
	}

	function hideBanner() {
		// hide cookie banner
		$(".cookie-banner").removeClass("visible");
		// remove marker from body
		$("body:first").removeClass("banner-activated");

		$('.background-zindex-fix').css('padding-top','0px');
	}

	function getCookie() {
		var name = "cookiesAccepted=", cookies = document.cookie.split(';');

		for (var i = 0; i < cookies.length; i++) {
			var cookie = cookies[i];
			while (cookie.charAt(0) == ' ') {
				cookie = cookie.substring(1);
			}

			if (cookie.indexOf(name) == 0) {
				return cookie.substring(name.length, cookie.length);
			}
		}
		return "";
	}

	function setCookie() {
		var now = new Date();
		now.setTime(now.getTime() + (365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000));

		var cookiestring = "cookiesAccepted=true;expires=" + now.toUTCString() + ";path=/";
		document.cookie = cookiestring; // Add the new cookie
	}

	$(".banner-close").on("click", function() {
		setCookie();
		hideBanner();
		enquire.unregister(enquireTopMarginFix.query);
	});



	/*
	 * Anpassung top-margin content bereich,
	 * da navigation + cookie banner position fixed
	 */
	//-----------------------------------------------------------------------
	function fixTopMargin() {
		var headerHeight = $('.cookie-banner').outerHeight();
		$('.background-zindex-fix').css('padding-top', headerHeight + 'px');
	}

	var throttleTs = null;
	function throttledResize() {
		if(!throttleTs) {
			throttleTs = setTimeout(function() {
				clearTimeout(throttleTs);
				throttleTs = null;
				fixTopMargin();
			}, 66);
		}
	}

	var enquireTopMarginFix = {
		query: 'screen and (max-width: 800px)',
		handler: {
			  match : function() {
					$(window).on('resize', throttledResize);
			  },
			  unmatch : function() {
					$(window).off('resize', throttledResize);
			  }
		}
	}
	//-----------------------------------------------------------------------


	if (getCookie() == "") {
		enquire.register(enquireTopMarginFix.query, enquireTopMarginFix.handler);
		showBanner();
	}

});

var vld = (function(){

    /* formerley triggered in footer.js
     resetBody = function(){
        var footElem=$('#footer');
        var bodyElem=$('#pagewrapper');
        var bodyHeight = bodyElem.outerHeight();
        var footHeight =footElem.outerHeight();
        var searchHeadHeight = $('#search-head').outerHeight();

        var wheight=window.innerHeight;
        if((wheight-footHeight)>bodyHeight){
          $('#pagewrapper').outerHeight(wheight-footHeight+"px");
          $('#search-head').outerHeight(searchHeadHeight+"px");
        }

    };
     */

    checkUndefind = function(variable){
        if(typeof variable === 'undefined'){
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    };

    isIe11 = function(){
        if (jQuery.browser.mozilla && parseInt(jQuery.browser.version, 10) === 11){
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    };

    vldAjax = function(params){

        var defaultParams={
            cache: false,
            type: "GET",
            showErrorMessage: true,
            data: {}
        };

        if(checkUndefind(params) || checkUndefind(params.url)){
            return false;
        }

        if((typeof params.showErrorMessage !== 'undefined') && (!params.showErrorMessage)){
            defaultParams.showErrorMessage = false;
        }

        $.ajax({
            url: params.url,
            cache: !checkUndefind(params.cache) ? params.cache : defaultParams.cache,
            type: !checkUndefind(params.type) ? params.type : defaultParams.type,
            data: !checkUndefind(params.data) ? params.data : defaultParams.data,
            success: function(result) {
                if(!checkUndefind(params.callbackForSuccess)){
                   params.callbackForSuccess.call(this,result);
                }
            },

            error: function(xhr, ajaxOptions, thrownError) {

                if (xhr.status === 401){
                    forceLogin();
                 }else if(xhr.status === 0){
                    //do nothing
                    //Firefox Bug! Permission denied to access property apply
                 }else{
                    if(!checkUndefind(params.callbackForError)){
                       params.callbackForError.call(this,result);
                    }else{
                        if(defaultParams.showErrorMessage){
                            showNotificationError("Es ist ein Fehler aufgetreten. Bitte versuchen Sie es später erneut.");
                        }
                    }
                 }
            }

        });
    };


    forceLogin = function(){
        window.location='/user/withLogin?isMobile=' + isMobile() + '&targetUrl='+encodeURIComponent(window.location.pathname);
    };

    noLazyloadForIe9 = function(){
	//ie9 hat kein Lazyload
        if($('html').hasClass('ie9')){
            $('.swiper-container img').each(function(index,element){
                if(typeof $(this).attr('src') === 'undefined' || $(this).attr('src').length === 0){
                    $(this).attr('src', $(this).data('src'));
                    $(this).removeAttr('data-src');
                }

            });
        }
    };

    isInView = function(elem) {

        var docViewTop = $(window).scrollTop();
        var docViewBottom = docViewTop + $(window).height();

        var elemTop = $(elem).offset().top;
        var elemBottom = elemTop + $(elem).height();

        return ((elemBottom >= docViewTop) && (elemTop <= docViewBottom));
    };

    lazyloadPatch = function(imgList){
        $(window).on("load scroll", function(){
            if((typeof imgList !== 'undefined' || imgList.length !== 0)){
                imgList.each(function(){
                    if(vld.isInView($(this))){
                         loadImg($(this))

                    }

            });
        }

    });
};

    loadImg = function(element){
        if((typeof element.attr('src') === 'undefined' || element.attr('src').length === 0)){
            var url = element.data('src');
            element.removeAttr('data-src');
            element.attr('src', url);

        }
    };

    addFadeinForSwiperBGImage = function(swiper){

        if (swiper.slides.first().hasClass("swiper-slide-fake")){
            var swiperSlideFake = swiper.slides.first();

              if(swiperSlideFake.length !== 0){
                var backgroundimageOverlay = swiperSlideFake.closest(".swipers").find(".backgroundimage");
                var fadeIn = "fade-in";
                if(!swiperSlideFake.hasClass("swiper-slide-active")){

                    backgroundimageOverlay.addClass(fadeIn);
                }
              }
        }
    };

    removeFadeinForSwiperBGImage = function(swiper){
        if (swiper.slides.first().hasClass("swiper-slide-fake")){
            var swiperSlideFake = swiper.slides.first();
            var backgroundimageOverlay = swiperSlideFake.closest(".swipers").find(".backgroundimage");
            backgroundimageOverlay.removeClass("fade-in");
        }
    };

    var modeldialogCallback, modeldialogCallbackArgs;

    initModaldialog = function(){
        var modal = document.getElementById('modal-dialog');
        var modalNoButton = document.getElementById('modal-no-button');
        if(modalNoButton !== null){
            modalNoButton.onclick = function(event){
                 closeModaldialog();
            }

            window.onclick = function(event) {
                if (event.target == modal) {
                    closeModaldialog();
                }
            }
        }

    };

    openModaldialog = function(callback){
        var args = arguments[0]
        modeldialogCallback = args[0];
       if(args.length > 1){
           modeldialogCallbackArgs = Array.prototype.slice.call(args,1);
        }
        var modal = document.getElementById('modal-dialog');
        if(modal !== null){
            modal.style.display = "block";
        }


    };

    closeModaldialog = function(){
        var modal = document.getElementById('modal-dialog');
        if(modal !== null){
            modal.style.display = "none";
        }

    };

    modaldialogYesAction = function(){
        modeldialogCallback.apply(this, modeldialogCallbackArgs);
        closeModaldialog();

    };

    showNotificationSuccess = function(message){

        noty({
            text : message,
            type : 'success'
        });
    };

    showNotificationError = function(errorMessage){

        noty({
            text : errorMessage,
            type : 'error'
        });
    };




return {

	noLazyloadForIe9 : function(){noLazyloadForIe9();},

    isInView: function(elem){ return isInView(elem);},

    lazyloadPatch : function(imgList){lazyloadPatch(imgList);},

    loadImg: function(elem){return loadImg(elem);},

    addFadeinForSwiperBGImage: function(swiper){return addFadeinForSwiperBGImage(swiper);},

    removeFadeinForSwiperBGImage: function(swiper){return removeFadeinForSwiperBGImage(swiper);},

    initModaldialog: function(){return initModaldialog();},

    openModaldialog: function(){return openModaldialog(arguments);},

    modaldialogYesAction: function(){return modaldialogYesAction();},

    vldAjax: function(params){return vldAjax(params);},

    checkUndefind: function(){return checkUndefind();},

    showNotificationSuccess: function(message){return showNotificationSuccess(message);},

    showNotificationError: function(errorMessage){return showNotificationError(errorMessage);},

    isIe11: function(){return isIe11();},

    //resetBody: function(){return resetBody();}
}
})();


(function(win, undefined){

  function isInstalled(b) {
    var a = false;

    if(b == undefined) {
      b = null;
    }

    try{
      var i = null, j = false;

      if(window.ActiveXObject) {
        try {
          i = new ActiveXObject("AgControl.AgControl");
          if(b===null || i.IsVersionSupported(b)) {
            a = true;
          }

          i = null;

        } catch(l) {
          j = true;
        }

      } else{
        j = true;
      }

      if(j){
        var k = navigator.plugins["Silverlight Plug-In"];

        if(k) {
          if(b===null) {
            a = true;
          } else {
            var h = k.description;

            if(h === "1.0.30226.2")
              h = "2.0.30226.2";

            var c = h.split(".");
            while(c.length>3)c.pop();
            while(c.length<4)c.push(0);

            var e = b.split(".");
            while(e.length>4)e.pop();

            var d,g,f=0;
            do{
              d=parseInt(e[f]);
              g=parseInt(c[f]);
              f++;
            } while(f<e.length && d===g);

            if(d <= g && !isNaN(d))
              a = true;
          }
        }
      }

    }catch(l){
      a = false;
    }

    return a;
  }

  win.silverlight = win.silverlight || {};
  win.silverlight.isInstalled = isInstalled;


})(window);
(function(w, undefined){

  // jtruncate bringt keinen eigenen kill switch mit....
  function killJTruncate(el) {
    var $el         = $(el),
        $moreLinkEl = $(el).find('.truncate_more_link'),
        $moreTextEl = $(el).find('.truncate_more'),
        $ellipsisEl = $(el).find('.truncate_ellipsis'),
        moreText    = $moreTextEl.html();

    $moreLinkEl.unbind('click');
    $moreLinkEl.closest('.more-div').remove();
    $ellipsisEl.remove();
    $moreTextEl.remove();

    if(moreText && moreText.length) {
      $el.html($el.html() + ' ' + moreText + ' ');
    }
  }

  // "cast & crew" (hat selbstgestricktes truncate)
  var schauspielerCustomTruncate = {
    $toggledElements: null,
    $toggleHandle: null,
    $toggleHandleLabel: null,
    hasTruncatableSchauspieler: false,
    initiated: false,
    firstRun: function() {
      var _ths = schauspielerCustomTruncate;
      if(!_ths.initiated) {
        _ths.$toggledElements = $('.schauspieler-display');
        _ths.$toggleHandle = $('.schauspieler-anzeigen');
        _ths.$toggleHandleLabel = $('.schauspieler-anzeigen>span');
        _ths.hasTruncatableSchauspieler = _ths.$toggledElements.length > 0  && _ths.$toggleHandle.length > 0;
        _ths.initiated = true;
      }
    },
    init: function() {
      var _ths = schauspielerCustomTruncate;
      if(!_ths.initiated) {
        _ths.firstRun();
      }

      if( _ths.hasTruncatableSchauspieler) {
        _ths.$toggledElements.addClass('none');
        _ths.$toggleHandleLabel.text('Mehr anzeigen');
        _ths.$toggleHandle.removeClass('none');
        _ths.$toggleHandle.removeClass('up');

        _ths.$toggleHandle.on('click', function(ev){
          ev.preventDefault();
            _ths.$toggledElements.toggleClass('none');
            _ths.$toggleHandle.toggleClass('up');
            var labelTxt = _ths.$toggleHandle.hasClass('up') ? 'Weniger anzeigen' : 'Mehr anzeigen';
            _ths.$toggleHandleLabel.text(labelTxt);
            return false;
        });
      }

    },
    kill: function() {
      var _ths = schauspielerCustomTruncate;
      if(!_ths.initiated) {
        _ths.firstRun();
      }
      if( _ths.hasTruncatableSchauspieler) {
        _ths.$toggledElements.removeClass('none');
        _ths.$toggleHandle.removeClass('up');
        _ths.$toggleHandle.addClass('none');
        _ths.$toggleHandle.unbind('click');
      }
    }
  }



  w.vlJqueryTruncateWrapper = {
    init: function() {
      // truncate inhalt/kritik
      $('.truncate').jTruncate({
        length: 595,
        moreText: "Mehr anzeigen",
        lessText: "Weniger anzeigen",
        ellipsisText: "...",
        minTrail: 100,
        moreAni: 0,
        lessAni: 0
      });
      // truncate inhalt/kritik
      $('.truncate-short').jTruncate({
        length: 480,
        moreText: "Mehr anzeigen",
        lessText: "Weniger anzeigen",
        ellipsisText: "...",
        minTrail: 100,
        moreAni: 0,
        lessAni: 0
      });

      // truncate cast&crew
      schauspielerCustomTruncate.init();
    },

    kill: function() {
      // truncate inhalt/kritik
      $('.truncate, .truncate-short').each(function(i, el){
        killJTruncate(el);
      });

      // truncate cast&crew
      schauspielerCustomTruncate.kill();
    }
  };

})(window);

initLazyLoad();(function(w){

    var resizer = function() {
        this.handlers = [];
        this.t = null;
    };

    resizer.prototype.handlers = [];
    resizer.prototype.throttleDelay = 10;

    resizer.prototype.add = function(fn){
        if(typeof(fn) === 'function') {
            this.handlers.push(fn);
        }
    };

    resizer.prototype.fun = function(ev) {
         var _ths = this;

         if (_ths.t === null) {
             _ths.t = w.setTimeout(function () {

                 _ths.handlers.forEach(function(h){
                    h.call(w, ev);
                });

                 w.clearTimeout(_ths.t);
                 _ths.t = null;

             }, this.throttleDelay);
         }
    };

    function resizerFn(ev, context) {

    }

    resizer.prototype.run = function() {
        var _ths = this;
        this.fn = function(ev) {
            _ths.fun(ev);
        };

        $(w).on('resize', this.fn);
    };

    resizer.prototype.stop = function() {
        $(w).off('resize', this.fn);
        w.clearTimeout(this.t);
        this.t = null;
    };

    resizer.prototype.reset = function() {
        this.stop();
        this.handlers = [];
    };

    w.viewportHandler = new resizer();
})(window);

setTimeout(function(){
$('.swiper-movie').each(function(){
    var $swiperWrapper = $(this),
        $swiperContainer = $swiperWrapper.find('.swiper-container'),
        $scrollbar = $('.swiper-scrollbar',this),
        // is dragging scrollbar
        isDragging = false,
        params = $.extend({
            nextButton: $swiperWrapper.find('.swiper-button-next'),
            prevButton: $swiperWrapper.find('.swiper-button-prev'),
            scrollbar: $swiperWrapper.find('.swiper-scrollbar'),
        } , swiperMovieDefaultParams), swiper;



    //-> 5x7 + bgimage
    if($swiperWrapper.hasClass('swiper-special')) {
        params.slidesOffsetBefore = 510;
        params.slidesOffsetAfter = 20;
        params.breakpoints = {
            1120: {
                slidesPerView: 6,
                slidesPerGroup: 6,
                spaceBetween: 10
            },
            930: {
                slidesPerView: 5,
                slidesPerGroup: 5,
                spaceBetween: 10
            },
            732: {
                slidesPerView: 4,
                slidesPerGroup: 4,
                spaceBetween: 10
            },
            532: {
                slidesPerView: 3,
                slidesPerGroup: 3,
                spaceBetween: 10,
                slidesOffsetBefore: 180
            },
            375: {
                slidesPerView: 3,
                slidesPerGroup: 3,
                spaceBetween: 10
            },
            350: {
                slidesPerView: 2,
                slidesPerGroup: 2,
                spaceBetween: 10
            }
        }
    }


    // init swiper
    swiper = new Swiper($swiperContainer, params);


    $scrollbar.on('mousedown', function(){
        isDragging = true;
    })
    .on('mouseup', function(){

        isDragging = false;
    })
    .on('mousemove',function (){

        if (isDragging)
        {
            swiper.lazy.load();
        }
    });

    //-> 5x7 + bgimage
    if($swiperWrapper.hasClass('swiper-special')) {

        // -> 5x7 (movie) + bgimage
        swiper.on('slideChangeStart', function(self) {
            $swiperContainer.addClass('darken');
        });
        swiper.on('onSlideChangeEnd', function(self) {
            if(self.isBeginning) {
                $swiperContainer.removeClass('darken');
            }
        });
        swiper.on('progress', function(self, progress) {
            if(!$swiperContainer.hasClass('darken') && progress > 0.01) {
                $swiperContainer.addClass('darken');
            } else if (progress < 0.01) {
                $swiperContainer.removeClass('darken');
            }
        });
        swiper.on('reachBeginning', function(self) {
            $swiperContainer.removeClass('darken');
        });


        // fix offset && positioning on window resize
        var $slide = $swiperContainer.find('.swiper-slide');
        function fixOffset(ev) {
            swiper.params.slidesOffsetBefore = Math.floor($slide.outerWidth() + swiper.params.spaceBetween);
            swiper.onResize();
            if(swiper.activeIndex === 0) {
                swiper.slideTo(0, 1);
            }
        }
        viewportHandler.add(fixOffset);
        fixOffset();
    }

});

var swiperSerieDefaultParams = {
    spaceBetween: 12,
    slidesPerView: 4,
    slidesPerGroup: 4,
    cssWidthAndHeight : true,

    //Scrollbar
    scrollbarDraggable: true,
    scrollbarHide: false,
    scrollbarSnapOnRelease: false,

    // Disable preloading of all images
    preloadImages: false,
    // Enable lazy loading
    lazyLoading: true,
    lazyLoadingOnTransitionStart : true,
    lazyLoadingInPrevNext: true,
    watchSlidesVisibility: true,

    observer: true,
    observeParents:true,

    //FreeMode
    freeMode: true,
    //freeModeSticky: true,

    breakpoints: {
        920: {
            spaceBetween: 10,
            slidesPerView: 3,
            slidesPerGroup: 3,
        },
        610: {
            spaceBetween: 10,
            slidesPerView: 2,
            slidesPerGroup: 2,
        },

        314:{
            spaceBetween: 10,
            slidesPerView: 1,
            slidesPerGroup: 1,
        }
    }
};

setTimeout(function(){
$('.swiper-serie').each(function(){
    var $swiperWrapper = $(this),
        $scrollbar = $('.swiper-scrollbar',this),
        // is dragging scrollbar
        isDragging = false,
        $swiperContainer = $swiperWrapper.find('.swiper-container'),
        params = $.extend({
            nextButton: $swiperWrapper.find('.swiper-button-next'),
            prevButton: $swiperWrapper.find('.swiper-button-prev'),
            scrollbar: $swiperWrapper.find('.swiper-scrollbar'),
        } , swiperSerieDefaultParams), swiper;

    // -> 16x9 (serie) + bgimage
    if($swiperWrapper.hasClass('swiper-special')) {
        params.slidesOffsetBefore = 311;
        params.breakpoints = {
            920: {
                spaceBetween: 10,
                slidesPerView: 3,
                slidesPerGroup: 3,
                slidesOffsetBefore: 311
            },
            610: {
                spaceBetween: 10,
                slidesPerView: 2,
                slidesPerGroup: 2,
                slidesOffsetBefore: 277
            },
            480: {
                spaceBetween: 10,
                slidesPerView: 2,
                slidesPerGroup: 2,
                slidesOffsetBefore: 228
            },
            370: {
                spaceBetween: 10,
                slidesPerView: 2,
                slidesPerGroup: 2,
                slidesOffsetBefore: 202
            },
            360: {
                spaceBetween: 10,
                slidesPerView: 2,
                slidesPerGroup: 2,
                slidesOffsetBefore: 198
            },
            320: {
                spaceBetween: 10,
                slidesPerView: 2,
                slidesPerGroup: 2,
                slidesOffsetBefore: 158
            }
        };
    }

    // init swiper
    swiper = new Swiper($swiperContainer, params);

    $scrollbar.on('mousedown', function(){
        isDragging = true;
    })
    .on('mouseup', function(){

        isDragging = false;
    })
    .on('mousemove',function (){
        if (isDragging)
        {
            swiper.lazy.load();
        }
    });

    if($swiperWrapper.hasClass('swiper-special')) {


        // -> 16x9 (serie) + bgimage
        swiper.on('slideChangeStart', function(self) {
            $swiperContainer.addClass('darken');
        });
        swiper.on('onSlideChangeEnd', function(self) {
            if(self.isBeginning) {
                $swiperContainer.removeClass('darken');
            }
        });
        swiper.on('progress', function(self, progress) {
            if(!$swiperContainer.hasClass('darken')  && progress > 0.01) {
                $swiperContainer.addClass('darken');
            } else if(progress < 0.01) {
                $swiperContainer.removeClass('darken');
            }
        });
        swiper.on('reachBeginning', function(self) {
            $swiperContainer.removeClass('darken');
        });



        // fix offset && positioning on window resize
        var $slide = $swiperContainer.find('.swiper-slide');
        function fixOffset(ev) {
            swiper.params.slidesOffsetBefore = Math.floor($slide.outerWidth() + swiper.params.spaceBetween);
            swiper.onResize();
            if(swiper.activeIndex === 0) {
                swiper.slideTo(0, 1);
            }
        }
        viewportHandler.add(fixOffset);
        fixOffset();
    }
});
},1);

// Manage history-back links
function historyGo (steps) {
  $('.player-close').off("click");
  $('.player-close').on("click", function(event) {
    //xt_click(this,'C', '0', 'Zurueck', 'N');
    window.history.go(steps);
    return false;
  })
  $('.history-back').off("click");
  $('.history-back').on("click", function(event) {
    //xt_click(this,'C', '0', 'Zurueck', 'N');
    window.history.go(steps);
    return false;
  });
}
historyGo(-1);

// ------------------- autocomplete starts here
if (window.location.search.indexOf("autocomplete") !== -1 || true == true) {
  $('.ui-autocomplete-input').each(function(index) {
    $(this).autocomplete({
      appendTo : $(this).closest(".ui-autocomplete-container"),
      delay : 200,
      minLength : 3,
      source : function(request, response) {
        if($.trim(request.term) !== ""){
        $.getJSON("/searchSuggestions?q=" + encodeURIComponent(request.term), function(data) {
          var array = data.error ? [] : $.map(data, function(product) {
            return {
              title : product.title,
              type : product.type,
              id : product.id
            };
          });
          var all = {
            title : "Alle Ergebnisse",
            type : "suche",
            id : request.term
          }
          if (data.length > 0) {
            array.push(all);
          }
          response(array);
        })
        }
      },
      focus : function(event, ui) {
        event.preventDefault();
      },
      select : function(event, ui) {
        event.preventDefault();
        var target = null;
        if (ui.item.type === "suche") {
          target = "/suche?q=" + ui.item.id;
        } else {
          target = "/" + (ui.item.type.toLowerCase().indexOf("movie") !== -1 ? "film" : "serie") + "/" + encodeURIComponent(ui.item.title.replace(/\/+/g, '')) + "/" + (ui.item.type.toLowerCase().indexOf("vodseason") !== -1 ? "x/" : "") + ui.item.id;
        }
        if (target !== null) {
          window.location = target;
        }
      },
      close : function(event, ui){

      if($("#short-search-form").length !== 0){
          $("#short-search-form").removeClass("opened");
          $("#short-search-form #start-search").removeClass("opened");
        }
      },
      open : function(event, ui) {
        autocomplete = $("#ui-id-1");
        autocomplete.css({
          // "width" : "320px",
          "left" : "0px",
          // "top" : "inherit",
          // "bottom" : "inherit",
          // "margin-top" : "65px",
          "border-top" : "1px solid #c5c5c5"
        });
        if($("#short-search-form").length !== 0){
          $("#short-search-form").addClass("opened");
          $("#short-search-form #start-search").addClass("opened");
        }

        if(document.getElementById("big-search-form") !== null) {
          autocomplete2 = $("#ui-id-2");
          autocomplete2.css({
            "width" : "580px",
            "bottom" : "inherit",
            "top" : "inherit",
            "left" : "0",
            "margin-top" : "6px",
            "z-index" : "10"
          });
        }

      }
    }).data("ui-autocomplete")._renderItem = function(ul, item) {
      var $a = $("<a></a>");
      $a.addClass("nowrap ellipsis overflow-hidden block").attr("title",item.title);


      if (item.type.indexOf("suche") !== -1) {
        $a.addClass("last").html(item.title);
      } else {
        var highlightedText = suggestionHighlightMatchedTextAsBold(item.title, this.term)
        $a.html(highlightedText);
      }
      return $("<li></li>").append($a).appendTo(ul);
    };
  });

'２':'2','６':'6','Ｂ':'B','Ｆ':'F','Ｊ':'J','Ｎ':'N','Ｒ':'R','Ｖ':'V','Ｚ':'Z','ｂ':'b','ｆ':'f','ｊ':'j','ｎ':'n','ｒ':'r','ｖ':'v','ｚ':'z','１':'1','５':'5','９':'9','Ａ':'A','Ｅ':'E','Ｉ':'I','Ｍ':'M','Ｑ':'Q','Ｕ':'U','Ｙ':'Y','ａ':'a','ｅ':'e','ｉ':'i','ｍ':'m','ｑ':'q','ｕ':'u','ｙ':'y','０':'0','４':'4','８':'8','Ｄ':'D','Ｈ':'H','Ｌ':'L','Ｐ':'P','Ｔ':'T','Ｘ':'X','ｄ':'d','ｈ':'h','ｌ':'l','ｐ':'p','ｔ':'t','ｘ':'x','３':'3','７':'7','Ｃ':'C','Ｇ':'G','Ｋ':'K','Ｏ':'O','Ｓ':'S','Ｗ':'W','ｃ':'c','ｇ':'g','ｋ':'k','ｏ':'o','ｓ':'s','ｗ':'w'};


function suggestiionGetGermanText (s) {
if (!s) { return ''; }
    var result = '';
    for (var i=0; i<s.length; i++) {
		result += text_map[s.charAt(i)] || s.charAt(i);
    }
    return result;
};

function escapeRegExp(str) {
  return str.replace(/[\-\[\]\/\{\}\(\)\*\+\?\.\\\^\$\|]/g, "\\$&");
}


function suggestionHighlightMatchedTextAsBold(str, q) {
  var tempQ = escapeRegExp($.trim(q).replace(/\s+/g, ' ').replace(/"+/g, '&quot;'));
  var tempStr = str.replace(/"+/g, '&quot;');
  var str_folded = suggestiionGetGermanText(tempStr).toLowerCase().replace(/[<>]+/g, '');
  var q_folded = suggestiionGetGermanText(tempQ).toLowerCase().replace(/[<>]+/g, '');
  var searchTextArray = q_folded.split(" ");
  var shouldHighlightedText = str_folded
  for (i in searchTextArray){
	  var re = new RegExp(searchTextArray[i], 'gi');
	  shouldHighlightedText = shouldHighlightedText.replace(re, '<$&>');
  }
  var spos = 0;
  var highlighted = '';


  for (var i=0,max=shouldHighlightedText.length; i<max; i++) {
    var c = tempStr.charAt(spos);
    var h = shouldHighlightedText.charAt(i);
    if (h === '<') {
       highlighted += '<b>';
    } else if (h === '>') {
       highlighted += '</b>';
    } else {
      spos += 1;
      highlighted += c;
    }
  }
  return highlighted;
}
}
(function() {
	// init
	function init() {

		var data = {

			serverUrl : "/footer",
			footerContent : $("#footer-inhalte")

		}

		if (data.footerContent.html().trim().length <= 0) {
			getFooter(data);
	//	}else{
	//		vld.resetBody();
		}


	}
    function getFooter(data) {
		vld.vldAjax({
			url : data.serverUrl,
			cache : false,
			type : "GET",
			callbackForSuccess : function(result) {
				data.footerContent.html(result);
				//vld.resetBody();
                jsbreakpoints.reInitFooter();
            }
		});
	}
    init();

})();

$('body').on("click", function(event) {

	if ($(event.target).closest('#short-search-form-container').length === 0) {

		_hideShow("#navigation", "off");
		$('#navigation').removeClass('search-opened');
	}

	if ($(event.target).closest('#navigation-link-1').length === 1) {

		var that = $('#navigation-link-1');
		var allClass = that.attr('class');
		if ($.inArray('navi-videoload', allClass.split(/\s+/)) == -1) {
			if (!that.hasClass("active")) {
				_switchHelper(HIGHLIGHTS_HEIGHT, "#sub-navigation-1", that);
				changeSubNavigationBackground("#sub-navigation-1");
				_helperXtClick('C', '1', 'Highlights', 'N');
				return false;
			} else {
				_closeNavigation();
				_inactiveAllSubMenues();
				return false;
			}
		}
	} else if ($(event.target).closest('#navigation-link-2').length === 1) {

		var that = $('#navigation-link-2');
		if (!that.hasClass("active")) {
			_switchHelper(FILMS_HEIGHT, "#sub-navigation-2", that);
			changeSubNavigationBackground("#sub-navigation-2");
			_helperXtClick('C', '2', 'Filme', 'N');
			return false;
		} else {
			_closeNavigation();
			_inactiveAllSubMenues();
			return false;
		}
	} else if ($(event.target).closest('#navigation-link-3').length === 1) {

		var that = $('#navigation-link-3');
		if (!that.hasClass("active")) {
			_switchHelper(TV_HEIGHT, "#sub-navigation-3", that);
			changeSubNavigationBackground("#sub-navigation-3");
			_helperXtClick('C', '3', 'TV', 'N');
			return false;
		} else {
			_closeNavigation();
			_inactiveAllSubMenues();
			return false;
		}
	} else if ($(event.target).closest('#navigation-link-4').length === 1) {

		var that = $('#navigation-link-4');
		if (!that.hasClass("active")) {
			_switchHelper(ABOS_HEIGHT, "#sub-navigation-4", that);
			changeSubNavigationBackground("#sub-navigation-4");
			_helperXtClick('C', '4', 'Abo', 'N');
			return false;
		} else {
			_closeNavigation();
			_inactiveAllSubMenues();
			return false;
		}
	} else if ($(event.target).closest('.trigger-ati').length === 1) {

		var that = $(event.target).closest('.trigger-ati');
		var str = that.data('aticlick').split(",");
		var Event = str[0].toString();
		var Level2ID = str[1].toString();
		var Name = str[2].toString();
		var Typ = str[3].toString();
		_helperXtClick(Event, Level2ID, Name, Typ);

	} else if ($(event.target).closest('#watchlist-navigation').length === 1) {
		return false;
	} else if ($(event.target).closest('#registration-container').length === 1) {
		return false;
	} else {
		_closeNavigation();
		_inactiveAllSubMenues();

	}
});

		_closeNavigation();
		_inactiveAllSubMenues();
		_closeTopNavigation();
	}

if (merkliste) {

	_rebuildWatchList = function(headCount,headLine) {
		var watchListCounter = 0;
		var elements = $('#search-result-list > .result-column');
		$(elements).each(function(index) {
			index++;
			var col = parseInt($(this).data("column"));
			if(!isNaN(col)){
				if ((index % col === 0) && (index !== 0)) {
					$(this).addClass("last");
				} else {
					$(this).removeClass("last");
				}
			}
		});
		var count = parseInt($(headCount).data("count"),10);
		if(!isNaN(count)){
			var newTitel = $(headLine).html().substring(0,$(headLine).html().indexOf("(")+1);
			newTitel = newTitel + (count-1)+")";
			$(headLine).html(newTitel);
			$(headCount).data("count",count-1 == 0?0:count-1);
		}
	}

	_removeItemFromList = function(movieId, type) {
		$('#search-result-list li').each(function(index) {
			var bvormerk = $(this).find(".bvormerk");
			var expectedMovieId = bvormerk.attr('data-movieid');
			var expectedType = bvormerk.attr('data-type');
			if (expectedType === "EPISODE") {
				expectedType = "MOVIE"
			}
			if (expectedMovieId === movieId && expectedType === type) {
				$(this).remove();
				$(window).trigger('scroll');
			}
		});
	}

	_updateWatchlist = function() {
		changeWatchlistElementsWithAjax();
		var searchMessage = $('#search-result-empty ');
		if (markedMovies == 0) {
			searchMessage.removeClass("none");
			$("#search-result-list").remove();
			$("#search-delete-all").css('visibility', 'hidden');
		} else {
			searchMessage.addClass("none");
		}
	}
}

// Utilities

function getCookie(name) {
	var i = 0;
	var suche = name + "=";
	while (i < document.cookie.length) {
		if (document.cookie.substring(i, i + suche.length) == suche) {
			var ende = document.cookie.indexOf(";", i + suche.length);
			ende = (ende > -1) ? ende : document.cookie.length;
			var cook = document.cookie.substring(i + suche.length, ende);
			return unescape(cook);
		}
		i++;
	}
	return null;
}

// Triggering the login box

function _setLoginCookie() {
	var cookieValue = "login=display; path=/; domain=" + host + ";";
	document.cookie = cookieValue;
}

function _deleteLoginCookie() {
	var ablauf = new Date();
	var gestern = ablauf.getTime() - (1 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
	ablauf.setTime(gestern);
	var cookieValue = "login=; path=/; domain=" + host + "; expires=" + ablauf.toGMTString();
	document.cookie = cookieValue;
}

function startLogin() {
	_closeNavigation();
	_inactiveAllSubMenues();
	_closeTopNavigation();

	var param = parseUri((document.URL));
	location.href = param.protocol + "://" + param.host + "/require-login" + param.path + "?isMobile=" + isMobile();
}

function _httpsUrl() {
	var urlend = location.href.length;
	var urlstart = location.href.indexOf(':') + 1;
	var httpsUrl = 'https:' + location.href.substring(urlstart, urlend);
	return httpsUrl;
}

function _switchHelper(HEIGHT, menue, active) {

	hideAllSubMenues();
	_closeNavigation();
	_closeTopNavigation();

	$(active).addClass('active');
	$(menue).show();
	handleNavigation(HEIGHT);
}

function _overlayOn() {
	overlay.appendTo('#pagewrapper');
}

function _overlayOff() {
	$('#overlay').remove();
}

function changeSubNavigationBackground(element) {
	var subNavigation = $(element).closest('#sub-navigation');
	var bgColorOld = $(element).data('bg-color-old');
	var bgColorNew = $(element).data('bg-color-new');
	subNavigation.removeClass(bgColorOld);
	subNavigation.addClass(bgColorNew);
	// additionally set equal heights to borders if exist
	var maxHeight = -1;
	$(element + ' ul').each(function() {
		maxHeight = maxHeight > $(this).height() ? maxHeight : $(this).height();
	});
	$(element + ' .border-block').each(function() {
		$(this).height(maxHeight);
	});
}

function _inactiveAllSubMenues() {
	$('#navigation-link-1,#navigation-link-2,#navigation-link-3,#navigation-link-4,#watchlist,#account').removeClass("active");

		_overlayOff();

}

function hideAllSubMenues() {
	$('#sub-navigation-1,#sub-navigation-2,#sub-navigation-3,#sub-navigation-4').hide();
	_overlayOff();
}

function handleNavigation(SUBHEIGHT) {
	$('#sub-navigation').animate({
		height : '+=' + SUBHEIGHT
	}, 100);
	_overlayOn();
}

function _closeNavigation() {
	$('#sub-navigation').animate({
		height : '0'
	}, 100);
}

/* Open the search field */
//SET CURSOR POSITION
//https://www.sitepoint.com/jqueryhtml5-input-focus-cursor-positions/
$.fn.setCursorPosition = function(pos) {
    this.each(function(index, elem) {
        if (elem.setSelectionRange) {
            elem.setSelectionRange(pos, pos);
        } else if (elem.createTextRange) {
            var range = elem.createTextRange();
            range.collapse(true);
            range.moveEnd('character', pos);
            range.moveStart('character', pos);
            range.select();
        }
    });
    return this;
};

$("#search-show").on("click", function(ev) {
	ev.preventDefault();
	_closeNavigation();
	_inactiveAllSubMenues();
	_hideShow("#navigation", "on");
	$('#navigation').addClass('search-opened');
	$('#display').focus();
	if($('#display').val().length > 0) {
        $('#display').setCursorPosition($('#display').val().length);
	}
	return false;
});

	return false;
});

/*
 * Event listener submit to validate if it is empty or not - only send form when
 * it is not empty
 */
$('#short-search-form, #big-search-form').submit(function() {
	var input = $(this).find(".real-input");
	if (input.val() === "") {
		return false;
	} else {
		return true;
	}
});

function _hideShow(container, direction) {
	if (direction === "on") {
		$(container + " .show-switch-to-hide").each(function(i) {
			$(this).hide();
			if ($(this).attr('id','short-search-form-container'))
			{
				$('div#logo-container').hide();
			}
		});
		$(container + " .hide-switch-to-show").each(function(i) {
			$(this).show();
		});
	}
	if (direction === "off") {
		$(container + " .show-switch-to-hide").each(function(i) {
			$(this).show();
		});
		$(container + " .hide-switch-to-show").each(function(i) {
			$(this).hide();
			if ($(this).attr('id','short-search-form-container'))
			{
				$('div#logo-container').show();
			}
		});
	}
}

// Helper -- gibt 'type' zurück

function _type(o) {
	return !!o && Object.prototype.toString.call(o).match(/(\w+)\]/)[1];
}

// vod AJAX interface

var changeSuccessCallback;

function onChange(event) {
	function _buildLogoutUrl() {
		currentUrl = location.href;
		var meinKonto =$('#newsletter-template').length>0 || $('#geraeteverwaltung-template').length>0 || $('#bezahlverfahren-template').length>0 || $('#erwerbsqualitaet-template').length>0

		if (meinKonto){
			currentUrl = location.origin;
		}
		var encUrl = encodeURIComponent(currentUrl);
		return "/session/logout?returnto=" + encUrl;
	}

	if (vod.userinfo.isLoggedIn() && vod.userinfo.getDisplayName()) {
		var color = $('#username').data("color");
		$("#username").html("<div class=\"" + color + " decoration-none nowrap ellipsis overflow-hidden block\" href=\"javascript:;\" id=\"account\" title=\"Konto von " + vod.userinfo.getDisplayName() + "\">" + vod.userinfo.getDisplayName() + "</div>");
	} else {
		$("#username").empty();
	}

	if (vod.userinfo.isLoggedIn()) {
		$("#logged-out").hide();
		$("#logged-in").show();
		$("#logout").attr("href", _buildLogoutUrl());
		deactivateStartLoginHandler();
		activatePowertip();
		activateLeftNaviagtion();

	} else {
		$("#logged-out").show();
		activateStartLoginHandler();
		activatePowertip();
	}

	$('.bvormerk').each(function(index) {
		var movieId = $(this).attr('data-movieid');
		var objectType = $(this).attr('data-type');
		if (objectType === "EPISODE") {
			objectType = "MOVIE"
		}
		var entityReference = {
			objectId : movieId,
			objectType : objectType
		};
		if (vod.userinfo.isEntityMarked(entityReference)) {
			$(this).addClass("active");
			$(this).data('powertip', 'Nicht Vormerken');
		} else {
			$(this).removeClass("active");
			$(this).data('powertip', 'Vormerken');
		}
		$(this).removeClass("ajax-loader");
	});
	if(event == "markedEntities") {
		amount.show();
		markedMovies = vod.userinfo.markedEntityCount();
	}
	if(event == "myMovies") {
		myMoviesamount.show();
		myMovies = vod.userinfo.myEntityCount();
	}
	if(event == "clearAllMarks") {
		markedMovies = 0;
		$("#search-result-list").empty();
	}
	changeView();

	if (changeSuccessCallback) {
		changeSuccessCallback();
		changeSuccessCallback = undefined;
	}

	// initialize the AJAX interface to request newsletter subscription related information
	try {
		// if newsletter page is addressed the following call succeeds (corresponding script is present and loaded) ...
		newsletter.load();
	} catch(error) {
		// ... , in other cases ignore the case the initialization could not be performed
	}
}

var onUserInfoError = function(errorCode) {
	changeSuccessCallback = undefined;
	onChange();
}

if (merkliste) {
	onUserInfoError = function(errorCode) {
		if (errorCode == "notLoggedIn") {
			// do nothing here right now
		} else {
			changeSuccessCallback = undefined;
			onChange();
		}
	}
}

function onMarkChanged(event, title) {
	if (event === 'mark') {
		noty({
			text : '<b>' + title + '</b> zur Merkliste hinzugefügt.',
			type : 'success'
		});
	} else if (event === 'unmark') {
		noty({
			text : '<b>' + title + '</b> von Merkliste gelöscht.',
			type : 'success'
		});
	} else if (event === 'error') {
		noty({
			text : 'Es ist ein Fehler bei der Bearbeitung der Vormerkungen aufgetreten. Bitte versuchen Sie es später noch einmal.',
			type : 'error'
		});
	}
}

vod.userinfo.setOnChangeHandler(onChange);
vod.userinfo.setAfterMarkChangedTrigger(onMarkChanged);
vod.userinfo.setOnErrorHandler(onUserInfoError);
vod.userinfo.load();

// end ajax interface

$(".bvormerk").on("click", function(event) {
	var movieId = $(this).attr('data-movieid');
	var objectType = $(this).attr('data-type');
	if (objectType === "EPISODE") {
		objectType = "MOVIE"
	}
	var entityReference = {
		objectId : movieId,
		objectType : objectType
	};
	var headCount,headLine;

	if (movieId && objectType) {
		$(this).addClass("ajax-loader");
		if ($("body").attr("id") === "watchlist-template" && $(this).hasClass("active")){
			var trommel = $(this).closest(".trommel");
			var swiperWrapper = $(this).closest(".swiper-wrapper");
			$(this).closest(".swiper-slide").remove();
			if(swiperWrapper.find(".swiper-slide").length == 0){
				trommel.hide();
			}

			if($(".trommel").length>0){
				headCount = trommel.closest(".head-count");
				headLine = trommel.find(".head-line");
			}else{
				headCount = $(".head-count");
				headLine = $(".head-line");
			}
		}

		changeSuccessCallback = function() {
			if (merkliste) {
				_removeItemFromList(movieId, objectType);
				_rebuildWatchList(headCount,headLine);
			}

		}
		vod.userinfo.markEntity(entityReference, !$(this).hasClass("active"));
	}
	return false;
});

function changeView() {
	$('#watchlist').attr('href', '/merkliste');
	if(typeof markedMovies !== 'undefined') {
		amount.text("("+markedMovies+")");
	}
	if(typeof myMovies !== 'undefined') {
		myMoviesamount.text("("+myMovies+")");
	}
	if(markedMovies !== undefined) {
		_updateWatchlist();
	}
}

function changeWatchlistElementsWithAjax() {
	if (vod.userinfo.isLoggedIn() && markedMovies !== undefined) {
		// $('#search-message').load('/user/movies/full #search-message .adjust');
		if(markedMovies == 0) {
			$('#search-message #markedMovies').text("keine Inhalte");
		} else if(markedMovies == 1) {
			$('#search-message #markedMovies').text("1 Inhalt");
		} else {
			$('#search-message #markedMovies').text(markedMovies + " Inhalte");
		}

	}

}

function activateStartLoginHandler() {
	deactivateStartLoginHandler();
	$('#bmerkliste').on("click", function(event) {
		startLogin();
		return false;
	});
	$("#account").on("click", function(event) {
		event.preventDefault();
		if (!($(this).hasClass("inactive"))) {
			startLogin();
		}
		return false;
	});
}

function deactivateStartLoginHandler() {
	$("#bmerkliste, #mymovies, #watchlist, #account, .product").off("click");
}

function activatePowertip() {
	$('#extras .powertip').powerTip({
		placement : 'w',
		mouseOnToPopup : 'true'
	});
}

$('#login,#open-login,.loginButton').on("click", function(event) {
	_helperXtClick('F', '9', 'Klick_Login');
	startLogin();
	return false;
});

function activateLeftNaviagtion() {

	$("#mymovies").attr("title", $("#mymovies > span").text());
	$("#watchlist").attr("title", "Meine Merkliste");

	$("#watchlist").on("click", function(event) {
		_helperXtClick('C', '7', 'Merkliste', 'N');
		return false;
	});

	$("#mymovies").on("click", function(event) {
		var targetlink = $(this).data("targetlink");
		location.href = targetlink;
		_helperXtClick("C", "7", "Gemietete_Filme", "N");
		return false;
	});
}

function _toggleBottomNavigation(trigger, navigation, HEIGHT) {
	if ($("#" + trigger).hasClass("active")) {
		_closeTopNavigation();
	} else {
		_closeTopNavigation();
		_openBottomNavigation(trigger, navigation, HEIGHT);
	}
}

function _openBottomNavigation(trigger, navigation, HEIGHT) {
	_closeNavigation();
	_inactiveAllSubMenues();
	_overlayOn();
	$('html').addClass('disable-scrollbar');
	$("#" + trigger).addClass("active");
	$('#navigation').animate({
		bottom : "+=" + HEIGHT
	}, 100);

	$('#' + navigation).animate({
		height : "+=" + HEIGHT
	}, 100).promise().done(function() {
		resetNavibarRightBlankWidth();
	});
}

function _closeTopNavigation() {
	_overlayOff();
	_inactiveAllSubMenues();
	$('#navigation').animate({
		top : '0'
	}, 100);
	$('#watchlist-navigation').animate({
		height : '0'
	}, 100);
	$('#registration-container').animate({
		height : '0'
	}, 100);
	$('html').removeClass('disable-scrollbar').promise().done(function() {
		resetNavibarRightBlankWidth();
	});
}


//Static Pages Menu
function getStaticMenu() {
  $.ajax({
  url: '/menu'
  }).done(function(markup) {
    $('.mainmenu ul').append(markup);
    $('.mobilemenulist').prepend(markup);
  });
}
window.getStaticMenu = getStaticMenu;

function VLComponent() {
	var _self = this;

	this.prototype.create = function(args) {
		this.args = args;

		return _self.component;
	}
};


if ($(".marginal").attr("id") === "floating-navigation") {
	$('.navi-block .toggle-silde').on("click", function(event) {
		var toggleSilde = $(this);

		if (toggleSilde.hasClass("less") == false){
			$('.navi-block .toggle-silde').removeClass("less").addClass("more");
			$('.sub-navi-block').each(function( index ){
				$(this).slideUp('fast', function() {
					$(document.body).trigger("sticky_kit:recalc");
				});
			} );
		}

		var subNaviBlock = toggleSilde.closest('.navi-block').find('.sub-navi-block');
		$(subNaviBlock).slideToggle('fast', function() {
			toggleSilde.toggleClass("more").toggleClass("less");
			$(document.body).trigger("sticky_kit:recalc");
		});
	});

	$('.navi-block .toggle-silde-text').on("click", function(event) {
		$(this).next().trigger('click');
	});

};

<script type="text/javascript">


$( document ).ready(function() {

    // darken background on scroll
    // --------------------------------
    if($(".htmlplayer").length>0){
        var $window = $(window),
            $document = $(document),
            bgSelector = '.background',
            $specialPageBg = $(bgSelector),
            style = document.createElement("style"), sheet;

        document.head.appendChild(style);
        sheet = style.sheet;
        // remove default darken style property
        $('body').css('background-color', 'black');
        sheet.insertRule(bgSelector + '::before { opacity: 0;}', 0);
        sheet.insertRule(bgSelector + '::after { opacity: 0;}', 0);

        $window.on('scroll', function() {
            var opacity = 0.5;
            if($window.outerWidth() > 701) {
                if ($(document).scrollTop() < 400) {
                    opacity = 1 - $document.scrollTop()/800;
                }
                $specialPageBg.css('opacity', opacity);
            }
        });

        $window.scroll();
    }
    // --------------------------------



    function changeVideoAudioListener(){
    	$(".player-swiper-container").find(".swiper-slide").each(function(){
    		$(this).on("click",function(){
    			var link = $(this).data("link");
          var poster = $(this).data("poster");
    			$(this).parents(".htmlplayer").find(".videoplayer").attr("src",link);
          $(this).parents(".htmlplayer").find(".videoplayer").attr("poster",poster);
          $(this).parents(".htmlplayer").find(".highlight").removeClass("highlight");
          $(this).find(".text").addClass("highlight");
    		});
    	});
    }
    changeVideoAudioListener();
});

</script>
